FAQ_A1 Sì. Siamo attualmente nella fase di beta testing privata con una beta pubblica da lanciare nel 2024. I nostri obiettivi sono basati sul mantenere le cose semplici, offrire valore e soprattutto un solido impegno nel mantenere la tua privacy e le informazioni personali al sicuro. Seguiamo le migliori pratiche al meglio delle nostre capacità, come design responsivo, piena accessibilità, supporto multilingue e per temi, gestione server veloce e sicura e molto altro.
FAQ_A2 Certamente! Offriamo un piano gratuito per chiunque abbia bisogno di registrare e monitorare i propri guadagni orari con funzioni aggiuntive correlate che rendono facile ottenere ulteriori approfondimenti. Vogliamo offrire questo livello di servizio gratuitamente perché è importante che tutti abbiano il maggior numero possibile di strumenti per aiutare a gestire il loro lavoro, i loro guadagni e rendere la loro vita più facile.
FAQ_A3 Pianifichiamo di fare il nostro primo milione con un modello di abbonamento pagato da aziende e manager che desiderano ancora più controllo sulle loro finanze. Il piano è semplice. Il piano a pagamento è una tariffa mensile fissa con dettagli da annunciare una volta rilasciato ufficialmente al pubblico.
FAQ_A4 Assolutamente! Il Calendario mostra le ore registrate per ogni giorno per lavoro. Clicca su un giorno per aprire il pannello Dettagli Lavoro per regolare come richiesto. La Dashboard mostra una suddivisione dell\'anno per mese, periodo di paga e lavoro.
FAQ_A5 Anche se attualmente forniamo i nostri servizi in 10 lingue, stiamo lavorando su altre! Torna spesso! Miriamo a fornire altre 15 entro la fine del 2024, con piani per aggiungerne altre col tempo. Se hai un suggerimento, siamo tutto orecchi! Merci! Salamat Po! Danke! :D
FAQ_A6 Ci impegniamo a essere il più aperti e chiari possibile su come gestiamo questo servizio. Abbiamo creato politiche che delineano i nostri termini di servizio, controlli sulla privacy e obiettivi di accessibilità.
FAQ_A7 Usa la nostra pagina Contatti per metterti in contatto con noi.

HELP_PAY_PERIODS_COMMON_MISTAKES_TITLE Common mistakes
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_COMMON_MISTAKE_1 Entering payday instead of the first workday in the pay period.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_COMMON_MISTAKE_2 Choosing the wrong anchor day, which shifts all later period boundaries.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_COMMON_MISTAKE_3 Saving before checking the preview labels against a real payroll record.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_FIGCAPTION Example: biweekly pay periods anchored to Monday, with the preview showing each period running through Sunday night.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_FULL_ALT Pay Period editor with biweekly frequency and Monday anchor day selected.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_GATHER_1 The first day of a real pay period from a recent pay stub or employer schedule.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_GATHER_2 How often you are paid: weekly, biweekly, semimonthly, or monthly.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_GATHER_3 The weekday your pay period starts on. Many field and shift schedules use Monday starts.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_GATHER_4 Whether you want a short editing grace period after a period ends.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_GATHER_TITLE What to gather before you start
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_INTRO Your pay period tells PayCal where one block of work ends and the next one begins. Get this right first, because your earnings summaries, pay-period labels, and reports all use it.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_POPOVER_ARIA Enlarged pay period setup example
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_POPOVER_CLOSE_ARIA Close pay period example
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_POPOVER_OPEN_ARIA Open enlarged pay period setup example
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_POPOVER_TITLE Pay period example
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_RECOMMENDED_TEXT If your schedule runs Monday through Sunday night and repeats every two weeks, use a Biweekly pay frequency, choose Monday as the anchor day, and enter a start date that is the Monday when a real pay period began.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_RECOMMENDED_TITLE Recommended setup for a typical two-week cycle
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEPS_TITLE Step by step
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_1 Open Settings and find the Pay Period section.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_2 Click Edit Pay Period to open the preview editor.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_3 In Pay Period Start, enter the first day of a real pay period. For a Monday to Sunday biweekly cycle, this should be a Monday.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_4 Choose your Pay Frequency. Most workers who are paid every two weeks should pick Biweekly.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_5 Set Pay Anchor Day to the weekday your periods start. Use Monday if your periods begin Monday morning and finish Sunday night.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_6 Pick an Editing Grace Period that matches how long you want recently ended periods to stay editable.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_7 Review the calendar preview. The P1, P2, and later markers should line up with your real schedule before you save.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_STEP_8 Click Generate to apply the new pay-period layout.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_THUMB_ALT Pay Period editor set to biweekly frequency with Monday anchor day and Monday-to-Sunday period preview.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_TITLE Setting up Your Pay Periods
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_UNSURE_DATE_TEXT Use the first day of the most recent completed pay period you can verify. Avoid using payday itself unless payday is also the first day of the work period, because many employers pay after the period has already ended.
HELP_PAY_PERIODS_UNSURE_DATE_TITLE If you are not sure which date to use

HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSERS_TEXT Use a modern, actively maintained browser. The following are known to work with WebAuthn passkeys:
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSERS_TITLE Supported Browsers
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_1 Chrome 100 or later (desktop and Android)
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_2 Safari 16 or later (macOS 13+ and iOS 16+)
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_3 Firefox 119 or later (desktop)
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_4 Edge 100 or later
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_5 Brave (based on a recent Chromium release)
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_BROWSER_CAVEAT Older browser versions, non-mainstream forks, and browsers with WebAuthn APIs disabled do not meet the minimum requirement.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_DECK What you need to use PayCal: a modern browser, a secure HTTPS connection, and a device that supports passkeys.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_HTTPS_TEXT WebAuthn only operates over a secure HTTPS connection. Attempts to register or authenticate over plain HTTP will be blocked by your browser. This protects the integrity of the passkey exchange and prevents credential interception.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_HTTPS_TITLE HTTPS and Secure Context
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_ITEMS_TITLE Supported authenticator types
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_ITEM_1 Built-in biometric: Touch ID, Face ID, Windows Hello, or Android biometric prompt
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_ITEM_2 Device PIN as a fallback when biometrics are unavailable
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_ITEM_3 Roaming authenticator: a hardware security key such as a FIDO2-compatible YubiKey
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_TEXT Your device must be able to create and verify a passkey. This is typically a built-in biometric sensor or device PIN, referred to as a platform authenticator.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_PASSKEYS_TITLE Passkey-Capable Device or Platform Authenticator
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_TITLE Browser & Device Requirements
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_UNSUPPORTED_TEXT Update your browser, switch to a supported browser, or use a device that meets the requirements above. PayCal will not silently downgrade to a less secure path.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_UNSUPPORTED_TITLE If You See an Unsupported Browser Warning
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_WEBAUTHN_TEXT PayCal uses the Web Authentication API (WebAuthn / FIDO2) for passkey registration and login. Browsers without WebAuthn support cannot complete registration or encrypted-data unlock. This is intentional and part of the security design.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_WEBAUTHN_TITLE WebAuthn Support Required
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_WHY_TEXT PayCal encrypts your data with a key that is unlocked by your passkey. Without a WebAuthn-capable device the encryption chain cannot be completed. These requirements reflect the cryptographic model that keeps your data private.
HELP_REQUIREMENTS_WHY_TITLE Why These Requirements Exist

HELP_TROUBLESHOOTING_PASSKEY_PROMPTS_3 Some browsers or OS versions require system-level authentication (fingerprint, PIN, Face ID) to be set up before passkeys work.

HELP_WEBAUTHN_DECK How WebAuthn, passkeys, and PayCal encryption work together to keep your data private.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_DEK_DESC The key that locks and unlocks your entry data.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_DEK_LABEL DEK (Data Encryption Key)
HELP_WEBAUTHN_KEK_DEK_TEXT PayCal protects your encrypted data with two keys. The DEK locks and unlocks entry data. The KEK protects the DEK when stored. Think of it as a key inside a lockbox.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_KEK_DEK_TITLE The KEK and DEK Encryption Model
HELP_WEBAUTHN_KEK_DESC A second key that protects the DEK when stored.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_KEK_LABEL KEK (Key Encryption Key)
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_ITEM_1 The DEK is used only when needed for active encrypt or decrypt operations.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_ITEM_2 Stored DEK material remains wrapped, not saved as plaintext key bytes.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_ITEM_3 Passkey-based unlock avoids relying on reusable password secrets for primary decrypt flows.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_ITEM_4 Unsupported browsers are blocked from passkey-required paths instead of being silently downgraded.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_TEXT The privacy goal is simple: reduce exposure of sensitive keys and keep encrypted data protected at rest.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_PRIVACY_TITLE What This Means for Privacy
HELP_WEBAUTHN_REQUIREMENTS_LINK See browser and device requirements
HELP_WEBAUTHN_TITLE WebAuthn Security and Privacy Controls
HELP_WEBAUTHN_UNSUPPORTED_TEXT Use a modern browser with WebAuthn support over HTTPS then retry registration or unlock.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_UNSUPPORTED_TITLE If You See an Unsupported Browser Warning
HELP_WEBAUTHN_WHY_TEXT PayCal uses passkeys for account registration and secure unlock. If your browser does not support WebAuthn you cannot complete passkey registration or unlock encrypted entries. This is intentional and part of the security design.
HELP_WEBAUTHN_WHY_TITLE Why PayCal Requires WebAuthn
HELP_WORK_HOURS_DECK Step-by-step guide to entering and validating daily work records.

HELP_WORK_HOURS_FULL_ALT Calendar work entry dialog showing editable rows for site and hours.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_GETTING_STARTED_TITLE Getting Started
HELP_WORK_HOURS_HOURS_TEXT Enter the total number of hours you worked (regular time). This includes any time you worked at regular pay rates. For example, if you worked 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM with a 1-hour lunch, enter 7.5 hours.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_HOURS_TITLE Hours
HELP_WORK_HOURS_LIVING_OUT_TEXT If you received a living-out allowance for this day (common for traveling workers), check this box. Your hourly rate and tax calculations will be adjusted accordingly.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_LIVING_OUT_TITLE Living Out
HELP_WORK_HOURS_OT_TEXT If you worked overtime hours, enter them separately here. Overtime is typically any hours beyond 8 hours per day or 40 hours per week, depending on your employment agreement.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_OT_TITLE Overtime (OT)
HELP_WORK_HOURS_OVERVIEW_TEXT PayCal.app makes it simple to track the hours you work each day across multiple sites. Whether you work at one location or several, entering your work hours is quick and intuitive. This guide will walk you through everything you need to know.

HELP_WORK_HOURS_SAVING_TEXT_2 You'll see a confirmation message once your work hours are saved successfully. Your calendar will update to reflect the hours you've logged.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_SAVING_TITLE Saving Your Work

HELP_WORK_HOURS_START_STEP_1_TITLE 1. Open the Calendar

HELP_WORK_HOURS_THUMB_ALT Work entry edit dialog with Add Entry, Save, and Close actions.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIPS_TITLE Tips & Best Practices
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIP_1 Enter Hours in Decimal Format: Use decimal points for partial hours. For example, 7.5 for 7.5 hours, or 8.25 for 8 hours 15 minutes.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIP_2 Log as You Go: If you work multiple sites in a single day, add a row for each site. You can track all sites under one calendar date.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIP_3 Double-Check Your Entries: Review your hours before saving, especially if you work irregular shifts. Errors can affect your pay calculations.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIP_4 Remember Special Situations: If you have living-out allowances or travel time that affects your pay, make sure to check those boxes.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TIP_5 Use Delete When Needed: If you accidentally enter hours or need to remove a work entry, you can delete that row. Click the delete button (often marked with an X or trash icon) to remove a row before saving.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TITLE How to Log Your Work Hours
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TRAVEL_TEXT If you had to travel to get to your work site, check this box. This may affect your calculations depending on your employment terms.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TRAVEL_TITLE Travel
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLESHOOTING_TITLE Troubleshooting Work Hours Issues
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_1_TEXT Check that you've filled in required fields: a site selection and at least one hour value (regular Hours or OT). Make sure your hour entries are valid numbers without letters or symbols.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_1_TITLE My Work Won't Save
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_2_TEXT Simply delete that entry and add it to the correct day. Click the delete button on the row, save, then navigate to the correct date and add the hours there.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_2_TITLE I Entered Hours for the Wrong Day
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_3_TEXT If you deleted a row before clicking Save, you can still undo it, just cancel out of the dialog without saving, and your deleted entry will still be there. If you've already saved, you'll need to re-enter the hours.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_3_TITLE I Accidentally Deleted an Entry
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_4_TEXT Try refreshing the page (Ctrl+R or Cmd+R). If the issue persists, clear your browser's cache and reload.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_TROUBLE_4_TITLE The Fields Look Jumbled or Misaligned
HELP_WORK_HOURS_VALIDATION_1 Site Selection Required: You must select a site for each work entry.
HELP_WORK_HOURS_VALIDATION_2 At Least One Hour Type: Enter hours in either the regular Hours field or the OT field (or both).
HELP_WORK_HOURS_VALIDATION_3 Valid Numbers: Hours must be valid decimal numbers (for example, 7.5 or 8, but not abc).
HELP_WORK_HOURS_VALIDATION_4 No Negative Hours: Hours cannot be negative.

TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_FACT_1 Le scansioni WCAG a livello di percorso vengono eseguite sulle pagine principali, inclusi controlli rigorosi del percorso per violazioni e controlli di contrasto più severi.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_FACT_2 Le regressioni di intestazioni e moduli vengono controllate in modo che la struttura dei punti di riferimento, le etichette e i messaggi di ripristino non si muovano silenziosamente.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_FACT_3 La copertura del riflusso e della spaziatura del testo controlla i percorsi principali con larghezze di visualizzazione compatte per individuare gli errori di overflow e spaziatura orizzontale.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_FACT_4 I test di regressione del percorso di navigazione e delle scorciatoie verificano i breadcrumb, i percorsi alternativi, il trasferimento del feedback, il comportamento di sicurezza delle scorciatoie e la copertura dei criteri delle scorciatoie da tastiera.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_FACT_5 I controlli a livello di suite combinano la copertura di Playwright, PHPUnit e della matrice del browser in modo che le dichiarazioni di accessibilità rimangano supportate dai test.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_ACTIVELY_VERIFY_TEXT_1 Ciò significa che le nostre dichiarazioni sull’accessibilità pubblica sono legate alla copertura effettiva della regressione, non solo alle intenzioni.

TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_AUDIT_CADENCE_TEXT_2 Ogni ciclo risulta in difetti monitorati (se rilevati), etichette di gravità, proprietari e un breve riepilogo dei rischi rimanenti.

TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_CONTINUOUS_PRACTICE_TEXT_2 Man mano che vengono controllati e coperti sempre più percorsi, continuiamo ad aggiornare questa pagina con le modifiche inviate, il lavoro aperto e l'approccio di verifica in modo che rimanga utile anziché generica.

TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEFECT_RESPONSE_TITLE Finestre di risposta ai difetti di accessibilità
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEPRECATION_POLICY_FACT_1 Non interrompiamo intenzionalmente il supporto per una combinazione verificata di browser e tecnologia assistiva senza prima pubblicare la modifica su questa pagina.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEPRECATION_POLICY_FACT_2 Quando una combinazione verificata diventa impraticabile da supportare a causa di vincoli di fornitore, piattaforma o sicurezza, riceviamo almeno un avviso con ciclo trimestrale prima che passi allo stato di massimo sforzo.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEPRECATION_POLICY_FACT_3 Qualsiasi avviso di deprecazione deve includere la combinazione interessata, il motivo della modifica, la data di entrata in vigore e l'alternativa supportata più vicina.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEPRECATION_POLICY_TEXT_1 Se una barriera segnalata influisce su una combinazione che attualmente verifichiamo direttamente, il problema viene valutato in base alle aspettative di supporto e non trattato come facoltativo.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_DEPRECATION_POLICY_TITLE Politica di deprecazione e modifica
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS_SAFETY_TEXT_1 Le regole di sicurezza della messa a fuoco impediscono ai gestori di scorciatoie e all'interfaccia utente con script di rubare la messa a fuoco mentre qualcuno sta digitando o lavorando all'interno di una modale. Le scorciatoie non vengono attivate all'interno dei controlli modificabili e le interazioni della finestra di dialogo mantengono l'ambito attivo fino alla chiusura della finestra di dialogo.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS_SAFETY_TEXT_2 Questa policy riduce la navigazione accidentale, protegge l'immissione di moduli e mantiene il comportamento della tastiera coerente con la copertura di regressione a livello di percorso che eseguiamo in Playwright e Lightpanda.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS_SAFETY_TITLE Focus sulla sicurezza
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_HOW_USE_ARIA_TEXT_1 Non trattiamo ARIA come un sostituto dell'HTML semantico. Utilizziamo ARIA per chiarire nomi di controllo, relazioni, descrizioni e modifiche di stato quando il solo HTML nativo non è sufficiente.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_HOW_USE_ARIA_TEXT_2 Ciò significa che il nostro lavoro sull’accessibilità va oltre il solo utilizzo di ARIA. Il comportamento della tastiera, le intestazioni, la gestione della messa a fuoco, il contrasto, il riflusso e il ripristino degli errori sono altrettanto importanti.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_HOW_USE_ARIA_TITLE Come utilizziamo ARIA
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_1 La navigazione principale supporta l'uso della tastiera, i collegamenti per saltare e le scorciatoie a tasto singolo documentate per le principali destinazioni.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_2 Le scorciatoie a tasto singolo (C, R, S, E, A, H, N, P, ?) sono documentate per i principali percorsi applicativi.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_3 Le scorciatoie non si attivano durante la digitazione di input o quando le finestre di dialogo sono aperte.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_4 I percorsi di aiuto e trasparenza forniscono più di un modo per navigare, inclusi i breadcrumb laddove ciò aggiunge un contesto utile.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_5 I percorsi controllati utilizzano punti di riferimento semantici, titoli chiari, etichette dirette e messaggi di stato laddove l'interazione necessita di feedback.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_6 I controlli dei token di contrasto e messa a fuoco sono automatizzati nel catalogo dei temi corrente e la matrice pubblicata viene tracciata nel nostro flusso di lavoro del backlog di accessibilità.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_7 Le impostazioni includono una preferenza tipografica adatta alla dislessia con spaziatura accessibile e supporto dello stack di fallback.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_FACT_8 I report sull'accessibilità possono ora iniziare da questa pagina e continuare nel flusso di contatti sicuri con i dettagli chiave precompilati.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_IN_PLACE_TEXT_1 Preferiamo prima l'HTML semantico e utilizziamo ARIA dove aiuta a esporre più chiaramente nomi, relazioni o modifiche allo stato in tempo reale.

TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_PAGE_TITLE Trasparenza dell'accessibilità
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_QUICK_KEYS_TEXT_1 PayCal pubblica scorciatoie a tasto singolo per le principali destinazioni in modo che i percorsi frequenti rimangano raggiungibili senza spostamenti ripetitivi del puntatore. L'attuale set documentato copre calendario, organizzazioni, impostazioni, guadagni, amministrazione, guida, note, periodi di pagamento e guida per le scorciatoie da tastiera.
TRANSPARENCY_ACCESSIBILITY_QUICK_KEYS_TEXT_2 Queste scorciatoie fanno parte del contratto di accessibilità solo quando sono prevedibili, documentate e si può ignorarle con sicurezza. Gli utenti possono continuare a navigare con collegamenti, pulsanti, collegamenti salta e punti di riferimento standard senza fare affidamento sulla memorizzazione dei collegamenti.

TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_CICD_GATE_TEXT_1 Every change must pass PHPStan Level 9 and the full PHPUnit test suite before it can be merged. Composer is locked to a deterministic dependency graph — no floating versions in production.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_CICD_GATE_TITLE CI/CD Controls
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_CVE_TEXT_1 Periodically GitHub Dependabot alerts flag packages as vulnerable. Before removing or replacing a package, PayCal verifies the specific CVE status at the installed version using the upstream advisory and the Packagist security database. During the 2026-05 audit, four moderate Dependabot alerts were raised. Investigation confirmed all four were already patched at the installed versions — no remediation action was required.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_CVE_TEXT_2 Separately, two packages — erusev/parsedown and yupmin/magoo — were found to have no usage anywhere in the codebase. They were removed regardless of CVE status. A package that is not loaded is a package that cannot be exploited.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_CVE_TITLE CVE Review and the 2026-05 Audit
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_DECK This page documents every PHP package PayCal directly depends on, why each is included, and the philosophy behind our approach to third-party dependencies.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_DEV_DEPS_TEXT_1 Dev-time dependencies are never loaded in production. The following packages are used exclusively during development and CI.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_DEV_DEPS_TITLE Development-Only Dependencies
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_HOW_TO_VERIFY_TITLE How to Verify
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_LAST_VERIFIED_LABEL Last verified:
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_NEXT_REVIEW_LABEL Next review due:
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PAGE_TITLE PHP Package Dependency Transparency
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_FACT_1 Every package must justify its existence by active call sites. A zero-reference dependency is removed regardless of CVE status.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_FACT_2 When a package is only used at one or two narrow call sites, we evaluate whether a first-party implementation is practical. Fewer moving parts means a smaller attack surface.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_FACT_3 All packages are pinned in composer.lock. No floating versions. No silent upgrades.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_FACT_4 Transitive dependencies are reviewed as a side effect of direct dependency audits. Orphaned vendor directories are removed.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_TEXT_1 PayCal's position on third-party dependencies is: use the minimum possible, own what is practical to own, and pin everything else. This is not a reaction to a specific incident — it is the standing default.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHILOSOPHY_TITLE Our Dependency Philosophy
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHPDOTENV_DECISION_TEXT_1 PayCal previously loaded .env files using vlucas/phpdotenv, a widely-used library with three transitive dependencies (phpoption, graham-campbell/result-type). The library was used at exactly two call sites: one safeLoad in the bootstrap file, and one load in config.php.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHPDOTENV_DECISION_TEXT_2 Rather than maintain an external dependency for 11 lines of active usage, we wrote PayCal\Infrastructure\Env\Dotenv — a 140-line first-party class that covers the full surface we actually use: KEY=VALUE parsing, blank and comment skipping, optional quote stripping, inline comment stripping, immutable mode, and a forceKeys override for specific keys (which eliminated a separate 20-line manual workaround that had existed in config.php for the SMTP password).
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHPDOTENV_DECISION_TEXT_3 The replacement removed 63 vendor files and three packages from the dependency graph. The public source for PayCal\Infrastructure\Env\Dotenv is available in the public repository.
TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_PHPDOTENV_DECISION_TITLE Why We Replaced vlucas/phpdotenv

TRANSPARENCY_PHP_PACKAGES_RUNTIME_DEPS_TEXT_1 These are the only PHP packages loaded in a production request. Each is justified by active usage and has no alternative that would reduce the dependency further without significant first-party engineering effort.
